\displaystyle\textit{prop-single-ic}(\textit{dst},\textit{val},\textit{rs})=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}[\textbf{qj}\mapsto\texttt{nil},\textbf{vj}\mapsto\textit{val},\textbf{qk}\mapsto\texttt{nil},\textbf{vk}\mapsto\textit{val}]\textit{rs}&\text{if }\textbf{qj}=\textit{dst}\wedge\textbf{qk}=\textit{dst}\\

[\textbf{qk}\mapsto\texttt{nil},\textbf{vk}\mapsto\textit{val}]\textit{rs}&\text{if }\textbf{qj}\neq\textit{dst}\wedge\textbf{qk}=\textit{dst}\\
\textit{rs}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

applies

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{exec}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{cyc}=\textbf{cpc}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\text{let }\textit{rsi}=\textit{rs-get-ic}(\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}},\textbf{rs-f})\quad\text{let }\textit{val}=\textit{comp-val}(\textit{rs},S)\quad\text{let }\textit{excp}=\textit{comp-exc}(\textit{rs},S)\quad\textit{rs-f}^{\prime}=\textit{prop-val-ic}(\textbf{dst}_{\textit{rs}},\textit{val},\textbf{rs-f})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}w]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto[\textit{rsi}\mapsto\\

\frac{\langle S,\textit{decode-detect-raw-ic}(\textit{fetch-n}(\textbf{imem},\textbf{pc},n))\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}i]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime},\emptyset\rangle\quad\langle S^{\prime},\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime}}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime\prime},\emptyset\rangle\quad\langle S^{\prime\prime},\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}w]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime\prime\prime},\emptyset\rangle\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,n\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}-\textbf{rs-f}]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}}]S,n\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf}

cache

B.4.
Formal Semantics of
MA-IC-N

B.4.1.
Transition System

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-N}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-N}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-N}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-N}}}\rangle
is a nondeterministic transition system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
.

B.4.2.
Semantics

The semantics of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
are broadly similar to that of

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC}}}
, except where nondeterministic choices are made. We define
the top-level transition rules for
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
below, and then discuss
the specific transition rules that differ. In general, the
nondeterminism is dealt with in the following way: the

stepall
transition rule involves the nondeterministic
selection of a number of instructions to fetch and issue, the
unavailable reservation station IDs
rs-busy?
, the set of ROB lines
which are allowed to commit during this cycle (if they were eligible
to be committed otherwise)
comm?
and the set of reservation
station IDs that are allowed to begin execution this cycle (if they
were eligible to begin execution otherwise)
strt?
. These
selections are referred to by the subsidiary transition systems that
make up
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
. Notice that some of the subsidiary transition
systems used to define
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC}}}
will be reused here, as their
behavior need not be modified.

\textbf{dst}\mapsto\textit{rb},\textbf{busy}\mapsto\texttt{true},\textbf{rb-pc}\mapsto\textit{ipc}]\textit{rs}]\textbf{rs-f}]S,Q^{\prime},\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-issue}

\frac{Q=\langle u,\textit{rb},\textit{dep1},\textit{dep2},\textit{ipc}\rangle\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textit{rs-needed?}(\textit{minst-op}(u))\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}i]{}\langle S,Q^{\prime},\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-issue-nors}

\mathcal{M_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e$}}}=\langle S_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e$}},\xrightarrow[\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e$}]{},L_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e$}}\rangle

is a transition system, where

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e}:S_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}\times\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muS}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}\times\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{R\mkern-2.0muS\mkern-1.0muI})

mop-time
,
check-barrier-start
and

check-memory-start
as defined in
Appendix
B.3.4
are used here.

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{qj}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\textbf{qk}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\textbf{exec}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{cyc}\leq\textbf{cpc}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}\langle S,Q^{\prime},\textit{strt?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-exec-wait-ready}

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{qj}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\textbf{qk}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\neg\textbf{exec}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\textit{barrier-op?}(\textbf{rs-mop}_{\textit{rs}})\quad\textit{check-barrier-start}(\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}},\textbf{rob})\quad\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\in\textit{strt?}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto[\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\mapsto\\
\textit{rs}]\textbf{rs-f}]S,\\
Q^{\prime},\textit{strt?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-exec-start-bar}

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{qj}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\textbf{qk}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\neg\textbf{exec}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\textit{memory-op?}(\textbf{rs-mop}_{\textit{rs}})\quad\textit{check-memory-start}(\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}},\textbf{rob})\quad\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\in\textit{strt?}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto[\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\mapsto\\
\textit{rs}]\textbf{rs-f}]S,\\
Q^{\prime},\textit{strt?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-exec-start-mem}

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{qj}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\textbf{qk}_{\textit{rs}}=\texttt{nil}\quad\neg\textbf{exec}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\neg\textit{barrier-op?}(\textbf{rs-mop}_{\textit{rs}})\quad\neg\textit{memory-op?}(\textbf{rs-mop}_{\textit{rs}})\quad\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\in\textit{strt?}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto[\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\mapsto\\
\textit{rs}]\textbf{rs-f}]S,\\
Q^{\prime},\textit{strt?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-exec-start}

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\vee\textbf{qj}_{\textit{rs}}\neq\texttt{nil}\vee\textbf{qk}_{\textit{rs}}\neq\texttt{nil}\vee\textbf{rs-id}_{\textit{rs}}\notin\textit{strt?}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,Q,\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}\langle S,Q^{\prime},\textit{strt?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf-exec-notready}

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-N}-{rs-f}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-N}-{rs-f}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-N}-{rs-f}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-N}-{rs-f}}}\rangle

is a transition system, where

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}-\textbf{rs-f}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}\times\mathbb{N}_{32}\times\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{R\mkern-2.0muS\mkern-1.0muI})\times\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{R\mkern-2.0muS\mkern-1.0muI})

decode-detect-raw-ic
is defined as in
Appendix
B.3.4
.

\frac{\text{Let }\textit{decoded}=\textit{decode-detect-raw-ic}(\textit{fetch-n}(\textbf{imem},\textbf{pc},n))\quad\langle S,\textit{decoded},\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}i]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime},\emptyset,\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle\quad\langle S^{\prime},\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime}},\textit{strt?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}e]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime\prime},\emptyset,\textit{strt?}\rangle\quad\langle S^{\prime\prime},\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}\text{-}\textbf{rs-f}\text{-}{}w]{}^{\ast}\langle S^{\prime\prime\prime},\emptyset\rangle\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,n,\textit{strt?},\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}-\textbf{rs-f}]{}\langle[\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto\textbf{rs-f}_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}}]S,n,\textit{strt?},\textit{rs-busy?}\rangle}\quad\textsc{rsf}

B.5.
Formal Semantics of
MA-IC-H

B.5.1.
Transition System

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-H}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-H}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-H}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-H}}}\rangle

is a deterministic transition system. States of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}}
are

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC}}}
states augmented with history information:

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\times H_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
.

H_{\texttt{MA-IC}}:\langle\textbf{comm-cy},\textbf{start-cy},\textbf{comm-cache},\textbf{ch-eff},\textbf{hist-lines}\rangle

•

\textbf{comm-cy}:\mathbb{N}_{32}
is the cycle during which the most recent commit occurred

•

\textbf{start-cy}:\mathbb{N}_{32}
is the first cycle for which this history state has data

•

\textbf{comm-cache}:\mathbb{N}_{32}\rightharpoonup\mathbb{N}_{32}
is the cache state, without any updates that may have occurred since the last instruction commit

•

\textbf{ch-eff}:\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}\rightharpoonup(\mathbb{N}_{32}\rightharpoonup\mathbb{N}_{32})
maps a ROB identifier to the cache entries that should be added to the cache after committing that ROB line’s microinstruction

•

\textbf{hist-lines}:\mathcal{SL}^{\ast}
contains information about the progress of all in-flight microinstructions

\mathcal{SL}:\langle\textbf{sl-rob-id},\textbf{sl-pc},\textbf{statuses}\rangle

•

\textbf{sl-rob-id}:\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}
is the ID of the ROB line that this status
information is for

•

\textbf{sl-pc}:\mathbb{N}_{32}
is the PC corresponding to the instruction
loaded into the ROB line with an ID equal to
sl-rob-id

•

\textbf{statuses}:\mathcal{S}^{\ast}
is the sequence of statuses
corresponding to this ROB’s progress

\mathcal{S}::=\texttt{fetch}\ \textit{pc}\ \textit{rsi}\ |\ \texttt{exec}\ |\ \texttt{wr-b}\ \textit{cache}\ |\ \texttt{delay}|\ \texttt{post-comm}

•

fetch

pc

rsi
where

\textit{pc}\in\mathbb{N}_{32}
and

\textit{rsi}\in\mathcal{R\mkern-2.0muS\mkern-1.0muI}\cup\{\texttt{nil}\}
indicates that the
microinstruction was fetched and issued. If

\textit{rsi}\neq\texttt{nil}
then it indicates the RS to which
this instruction was issued. If
\textit{rsi}=\texttt{nil}
, the
microinstruction does not require a RS.
pc
indicates the
PC of the instruction corresponding to the microinstruction that was
fetched and issued.

•

exec
indicates that the microinstruction was executing
in an RS.

•

wr-b

cache
where

\textit{cache}\in\mathbb{N}_{32}\rightharpoonup\mathbb{N}_{32}
indicates that the
instruction wrote back, and
cache
indicates the value of
the cache at the time of the write back.

•

delay
indicates that the microinstruction either had
not started execution because it was waiting on a dependency, or it
had completed execution and written back to the ROB, but the ROB
line had not yet been committed as another in-flight instruction
that comes earlier in program order had not yet been committed.

•

post-comm
indicates that the microinstruction is a

mem-check
or
memi-check
microinstruction that has
been committed before its corresponding
mldr
or

mldri
instruction. This is the only case where a
microinstruction’s status line is retained after it is retired.

The history information gathered by
MA-IC-H
will be used to
determine whether an arbitrary
MA-IC-H
state is “entangled”, where
“entangled” means essentially that when the state is invalidated back to
the point at which the earliest in-flight instruction was issued and
run forward, it is possible to reach that state. All invalidated
states are considered entangled, so the only states that we need to worry
about here are those that have in-flight instructions (a nonempty
pipeline).

\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}]{}
treats the first component of the state in the
same way that
MA-IC
does. That is:

\langle s,h\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}]{}\langle s^{\prime},h^{\prime}\rangle\implies s\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}]{}s^{\prime}

Let
\textit{will-commit?-ic}:S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\rightarrow\mathbb{B}
be a function
that returns
true
iff
rob
is nonempty and given
q
is the first
ROB line,
\textbf{rdy}_{q}
. This indicates that at least one
microinstruction will be committed on the next cycle.

Let

\textit{to-commit-ic}:\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}\rightarrow\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}

be a function that returns the sequence of ROB lines that will be
committed in the next step.

\textit{to-commit-ic}(\sigma)=\langle\sigma_{k}\rangle_{k<\max(S)}
, where:

\displaystyle S=\{

\displaystyle i\in\mathrm{dom}(\sigma)\colon

\displaystyle\langle\forall x\colon x\in\mathrm{dom}(\sigma)\wedge x<i-1\colon\textbf{rdy}_{\sigma(x)}\wedge\neg\textbf{excep}_{\sigma(x)}\wedge

\displaystyle\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\sigma(x)}\notin\{\texttt{mhalt},\texttt{mjge},\texttt{mjg}\}\rangle\wedge

\displaystyle(\neg\textbf{rdy}_{\sigma(i)}\vee\textbf{excep}_{\sigma(i)}\vee\textbf{rob-mop}_{\sigma(i)}\in\{\texttt{mhalt},\texttt{mjge},\texttt{mjg}\}\vee

\displaystyle i=\max(\mathrm{dom}(\sigma)))\}

Let
\textit{will-invld?-ic}:\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}\rightarrow\mathbb{B}
be a
function that returns
true
iff a microinstruction will be committed
that will result in an invalidation. Note that it is defined to only
check the last element of
\textit{to-commit-ic}(\sigma)
, as the
definition of
to-commit-ic
is such that if the returned
sequence contains an invalidation, it will always be the final element
of the sequence.

\displaystyle\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\sigma)\iff

\displaystyle\pi\neq\emptyset\wedge(\textbf{excep}_{\pi(i)}\vee\textbf{rob-mop}_{\pi(i)}\in\{\texttt{mhalt},\texttt{mjge},\texttt{mjg}\})

\displaystyle\text{ where }\pi=\textit{to-commit-ic}(\sigma),i=\max(\mathrm{dom}(\pi))

\frac{\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}]{}\langle S,H\rangle}\quad\textsc{ic-h-halted}

\frac{\neg\textbf{halt}\quad S\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}]{}S^{\prime}\quad\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{comm-cy}]{}\langle S,\langle...,\textbf{comm-cy}^{\prime},...\rangle\rangle\quad\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c]{}\langle S,\langle...,\textbf{comm-cache}^{\prime},...\rangle\rangle\quad\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{ch-eff}]{}\langle S,\langle...,\textbf{ch-eff}^{\prime},...\rangle\rangle\quad\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}]{}\langle S,\langle...,\textbf{hist-lines}^{\prime},...\rangle\rangle\quad\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{start-cy}]{}\langle S,\langle...,\textbf{start-cy}^{\prime},...\rangle\rangle}{S\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}]{}\langle S^{\prime},[\textbf{comm-cy}\mapsto\textbf{comm-cy}^{\prime},\\
\textbf{comm-cache}\mapsto\textbf{comm-cache}^{\prime},\textbf{ch-eff}\mapsto\textbf{ch-eff}^{\prime},\\
\textbf{hist-lines}\mapsto\textbf{hist-lines}^{\prime},\textbf{start-cy}\mapsto\textbf{start-cy}^{\prime}]H\rangle}\quad\textsc{ic-h-stepall}

Note that in the below transition systems, it’s not necessary to
handle ROB entries corresponding to ready jumps, ready halts, or ready
entries where the exception flag is set since the history is going to
be invalidated in those cases anyways. So, the below rules are not
going to handle those cases.

comm-cy

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{comm-cy}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{comm-cy}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-H}-{comm-cy}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{comm-cy}}}\rangle

is a transition
system.
S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{comm-cy}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}
.

\frac{\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\textit{will-commit?-ic}(S)}{\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{comm-cy}]{}\langle S,[\textbf{comm-cy}\mapsto\textbf{cyc}]H\rangle}\quad\textsc{ccyc-will-commit}

\frac{\textbf{halt}\vee\neg\textit{will-commit?-ic}(S)}{\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{comm-cy}]{}\langle S,H\rangle}\quad\textsc{ccyc-will-not-commit}

comm-cache

\mathcal{M_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c$}}}=\langle S_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c$}},\xrightarrow[\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c$}]{},L_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c$}}\rangle

is a transition
system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\times\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}
.

\frac{Q=\textit{rl}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rl}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rl}}\in\{\texttt{mldri},\texttt{mldr}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\text{let }\textit{eff}=\textit{get}_{\textbf{ch-eff}}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rl}},\emptyset)}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c]{}\\
\langle\langle S,[\textbf{comm-cache}\mapsto\textbf{comm-cache}\cup\textit{eff}]H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{commit-cache-commit}

\frac{Q=\textit{rl}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rl}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rl}}\notin\{\texttt{mldri},\texttt{mldr}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{commit-cache-rdy}

\frac{Q=\textit{rl}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rl}}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ccmem}\text{-}{}c]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,\emptyset\rangle}\quad\textsc{commit-cache-notrdy}

ch-eff

\mathcal{M_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c$}}}=\langle S_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c$}},\xrightarrow[\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c$}]{},L_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c$}}\rangle

is a transition
system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\times\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}
.

\frac{Q=\textit{rl}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rl}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rl}}\neq\texttt{mhalt}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c]{}\\
\langle\langle S,[\textbf{ch-eff}\mapsto[\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rl}}\mapsto\uparrow]\textbf{ch-eff}]H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{cache-effects-committed-rm}

\frac{Q=\textit{rl}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rl}}\vee\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rl}}=\texttt{mhalt}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{cache-effects-committed-other}

\mathcal{M_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w$}}}=\langle S_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w$}},\xrightarrow[\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w$}]{},L_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w$}}\rangle
is a transition
system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\times\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muS}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}
.

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\quad\textbf{cpc}_{\textit{rs}}=\textbf{cyc}\quad\textbf{rs-mop}_{\texttt{rs}}\in\{\texttt{mldri},\texttt{mldr}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\text{let }\textit{ea}=\textbf{vj}_{\textit{rs}}\oplus\textbf{vk}_{\textit{rs}}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w]{}\\
\langle\langle S,[\textbf{ch-eff}\mapsto[\textbf{dst}_{\textit{rs}}\mapsto[\textit{ea}\mapsto\textbf{dmem}(\textit{ea})]]\textbf{ch-eff}]H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{cache-effects-wr-b-ldr}

\frac{Q=\textit{rs}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textbf{busy}_{\textit{rs}}\vee\textbf{cpc}_{\textit{rs}}\neq\textbf{cyc}\vee\textbf{rs-mop}_{\texttt{rs}}\notin\{\texttt{mldri},\texttt{mldr}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{cache-effects-wr-b-no-ldr}

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{ch-eff}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{ch-eff}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-H}-{ch-eff}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-H}-{ch-eff}}}\rangle

is a transition
system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{ch-eff}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}
.

\frac{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,\textbf{rob}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}c]{}^{\ast}\langle\langle x,H^{\prime}\rangle,\emptyset\rangle\quad\langle\langle S,H^{\prime}\rangle,\textbf{rs-f}\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{ceff}\text{-}{}w]{}^{\ast}\langle\langle z,H^{\prime\prime}\rangle,\emptyset\rangle\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle S,H\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}-\textbf{ch-eff}]{}\langle S,[\textbf{ch-eff}\mapsto\textbf{ch-eff}_{H^{\prime\prime}}]H\rangle}\quad\textsc{cache-effects}

hist-lines

\mathcal{M_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}\text{-}{}rm$}}}=\langle S_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}\text{-}{}rm$}},\xrightarrow[\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}\text{-}{}rm$}]{},L_{\text{$\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}\text{-}{}rm$}}\rangle

is a transition system.
S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{hl}\text{-}{}rm}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\times\mathcal{RBL}_{\textit{IC}}^{\ast}
.

\textit{rm-hist-line}:\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}\times\mathcal{SL}^{\ast}\rightarrow\mathcal{SL}^{\ast}
is a function that removes any status line in the
given sequence that has the given ROB ID.

\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textit{id},\sigma)=\pi

where for
A=\{i\in\mathrm{dom}(\sigma)\colon\textbf{sl-rob-id}_{\sigma(i)}\neq\textit{id}\}
and
\tau
such that
\tau
is a sequence consisting of the elements of
A
in monotonically increasing order,

\langle\forall i\colon i\in\mathrm{dom}(\tau)\colon\pi(i)=\sigma(\tau(i))\rangle

\frac{Q=\textit{rb}\bullet\textit{rb}^{\prime}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}}\quad\neg\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}^{\prime}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rb}}\in\{\texttt{mem-check},\texttt{memi-check}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{sc}\text{-}{}rm]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,\emptyset\rangle}\quad\textsc{lines-rm-commit-partial-ldr}

\frac{Q=\textit{rb}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rb}}\in\{\texttt{ldr},\texttt{ldri}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\text{Let }H^{\prime}=[\textbf{hist-lines}\mapsto\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textit{prev}_{\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rb}}),\quad\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rb}},\textbf{hist-lines}))]H}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{sc}\text{-}{}rm]{}\langle\langle S,H^{\prime}\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{lines-rm-commit-complete-ldr}

\frac{Q=\textit{rb}\bullet\textit{rb}^{\prime}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}^{\prime}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rb}}\in\{\texttt{mem-check},\texttt{memi-check}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\text{Let }H^{\prime}=[\textbf{hist-lines}\mapsto\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rb}^{\prime}},\quad\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rb}},\textbf{hist-lines}))]H}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{sc}\text{-}{}rm]{}\langle\langle S,H^{\prime}\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{lines-rm-commit-both-ldr}

\frac{Q=\textit{rb}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}}\quad\textbf{rob-mop}_{\textit{rb}}\notin\{\texttt{mem-check},\texttt{memi-check},\texttt{ldr},\texttt{ldri}\}\quad\neg\textit{will-invld?-ic}(\textbf{rob})\quad\neg\textbf{halt}\quad\text{Let }H^{\prime}=[\textbf{hist-lines}\mapsto\textit{rm-hist-line}(\textbf{rob-id}_{\textit{rb}},\textbf{hist-lines})]H}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{sc}\text{-}{}rm]{}\langle\langle S,H^{\prime}\rangle,Q^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{lines-rm-commit-no-invld}

\frac{Q=\textit{rb}\bullet Q^{\prime}\quad\neg\textbf{rdy}_{\textit{rb}}\quad\neg\textbf{halt}}{\langle\langle S,H\rangle,Q\rangle\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}\text{-}\textit{sc}\text{-}{}rm]{}\langle\langle S,H\rangle,\emptyset\rangle}\quad\textsc{lines-rm-commit-notrdy}

start-cy

As can be seen in Section
B.5.1
, there are three
situations in which the cycle at which the earliest microinstruction
in
hist-lines
was issued may change: (1) if the sequence of lines is
empty and a microinstruction is issued, (2) if a microinstruction is
committed, and (3) if the MA is invalidated.

\textit{sc-rem-rb}:\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}\times\mathbb{N}_{32}\times\mathcal{S}^{\ast}\rightarrow\mathbb{N}_{32}
is a function that
determines what the start cycle should be after removing the history
line corresponding to the given ROB ID. If the history line to be
removed is the oldest in the history (the first entry) and there are
at least two history lines, the start cycle must be adjusted by the
difference in cycles between the cycle during which the
microinstruction corresponding to the first entry was issued and the
cycle during which the microinstruction corresponding to the second
entry was issued.

\displaystyle\textit{sc-rem-rb}(\textit{id},\textit{cy},\sigma)=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\textit{cy}&\text{if }\sigma=\emptyset\\
\textit{cy}&\text{if }\textbf{sl-rob-id}_{\sigma(1)}\neq\textit{id}\\
0&\text{if }\textbf{sl-rob-id}_{\sigma(1)}=\textit{id}\wedge|\sigma|=1\\
\textit{cy}\oplus(|\textbf{statuses}_{\sigma(1)}|\ominus|\textbf{statuses}_{\sigma(2)}|)&\text{if }\textbf{sl-rob-id}_{\sigma(1)}=\textit{id}\wedge|\sigma|>1\\
\end{cases}

B.6.
Formal Semantics of
MA-IC-A

B.6.1.
Transition System

\mathcal{M_{\text{{MA-IC-A}}}}=\langle S_{\text{{MA-IC-A}}},A_{\text{{MA-IC-A}}},\xrightarrow[\text{{MA-IC-A}}]{},L_{\text{{MA-IC-A}}}\rangle
is an action labeled transition system.

S_{\texttt{MA-IC-A}}=S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
.

B.6.2.
Semantics

\frac{\textbf{halt}}{S\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-A}]{\emptyset}S}\quad\textsc{halted}

\frac{\neg\textbf{halt}\quad S\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}]{}S^{\prime}\quad a=\textit{auth-actions}(S,S^{\prime})}{S\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-A}]{a}S^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{stepall}

Appendix C
Meltdown Proof Obligations

We will now describe the proof obligations that arise from using our
notion of correctness for Meltdown on
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
and

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC}}}
. First, we will instantiate the set of entangled states
with
\mathcal{X}=\texttt{MA-IC}
. We use the formal definition from
Section
4.2
, which requires that we provide

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
,
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}}
,
\textit{step-using-h}_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}
,

\textit{invl}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
,
\textit{init-h}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
and

S^{\textit{init}}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}
. We briefly discussed
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
and

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}}
above and full definitions can be found in
Appendices
B.4
and
B.5

respectively. The rest of the functions are defined below.

\textit{reset-rs}(\textit{rs})=[\textbf{busy}\mapsto\texttt{false},\textbf{exec}\mapsto\texttt{false}]\textit{rs}

\textit{reset-rs-f}(\sigma)=\pi\text{ such that }\langle\forall i\colon i\in\mathrm{dom}(\sigma)\colon\pi(i)=\textit{reset-rs}(\sigma(i))\rangle

\textit{comp-start-cyc}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}(s,h)=\begin{cases}\textbf{cyc}&\text{if }|\textbf{hist-lines}|=0\\
\textbf{start-cy}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\textit{invl}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}(s,h)

\displaystyle=[

\displaystyle\textbf{fetch-pc}\mapsto\textbf{pc},

\displaystyle\textbf{rob}\mapsto\emptyset,

\displaystyle\textbf{reg-st}\mapsto\emptyset,

\displaystyle\textbf{rs-f}\mapsto\textit{reset-rs-f}(\textbf{rs-f})

\displaystyle\textbf{cache}\mapsto\textbf{comm-cache}

\displaystyle\textbf{cyc}\mapsto\textit{comp-start-cyc}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}(s,h)

\displaystyle]s

\textit{init-h}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}(s)=\langle\textbf{cyc}_{s},\textbf{cyc}_{s},\emptyset,\emptyset,\emptyset\rangle

\displaystyle S^{\textit{init}}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}=\{s\in

\displaystyle S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\colon\textbf{fetch-pc}_{s}=\textbf{pc}_{s}\wedge\textbf{rob}_{s}=\emptyset\wedge\textbf{reg-st}_{s}=\emptyset\wedge

\displaystyle\langle\forall i\colon i\in\mathrm{dom}(\textbf{rs-f})\colon\neg\textbf{busy}_{\textbf{rs-f}(i)}\wedge\neg\textbf{exec}_{\textbf{rs-f}(i)}\rangle\}

\textit{steps-to-take}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}(\langle s,h\rangle)=\begin{cases}0&\text{if }\textbf{hist-lines}_{h}=\emptyset\\
\textbf{cyc}_{s}\ominus\textbf{start-cy}_{h}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\textit{step-using-h}_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}
operates by calculating the
appropriate values for
n
,
comm?
,
strt?
and

rs-busy?
, and then using the
stepall
transition rule for

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
with those values.

Let

\textit{get-h}:H_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\times\mathbb{N}_{32}\rightarrow(\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}\times\mathbb{N}_{32}\times\mathcal{S})^{\ast}
be a function that given
history information and a cycle, gets a sequence of tuples, where each
tuple describes the status of one of the ROB lines during the given
cycle.

n
can be calculated for a state
\langle s,h\rangle
by counting the
number of ROB lines with a
fetch
status in

\textit{get-h}(h,\textbf{cyc}_{s})
.

rs-busy?
can be calculated for a state
\langle s,h\rangle
by computing

\mathcal{R\mkern-2.0muS\mkern-1.0muI}\setminus\mathit{used}
where
\mathit{used}
is a set
computed by taking all of the
fetch
status in

\textit{get-h}(h,\textbf{cyc}_{s})
, selecting only those statuses that
indicate an assignment to an RS, and collecting the RS IDs from such
statuses.

comm?
can be calculated for a state
\langle s,h\rangle
by
computing
\mathcal{R\mkern-1.0muB}\setminus\mathit{used}
where
\mathit{used}
is a
set containing all of the ROB IDs in
\textit{get-h}(h,\textbf{cyc}_{s})
.

strt?
can be calculated for a state
\langle s,h\rangle
by
finding all of the reservation stations
\mathit{rs}
in
\textbf{rs-f}_{s}
such that

\textbf{busy}_{\mathit{rs}}
and
\textbf{dst}_{\mathit{rs}}
is one of the ROB
IDs that has a
exec
status in

\textit{get-h}(h,\textbf{cyc}_{s})
, and then collecting the
rs-id
for
all such RSes.

Then, we get that:

\displaystyle S^{\textit{ent}}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}=\{

\displaystyle\langle s,h\rangle\in S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}},i=\textit{steps-to-take}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}(\langle s,h\rangle)\colon

\displaystyle\langle\exists h\colon h^{\prime}\in H\colon\textit{step-using-h}^{i}_{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}(\textit{invl}_{\mathcal{X}}(s,h),h)=\langle s,h^{\prime}\rangle\rangle\}

We are claiming that
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
,
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC}}}
,
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}
and

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}}
are all TRSes. This means that they must all be well-typed
and left-total, as is required by the definition of a TRS.

From the use of the definition of the set of entangled states, we now
must discharge the following obligations:

(24)

\langle\forall s,u\colon s,u\in S_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\wedge s\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC}]{}u\colon s\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-N}]{}u\rangle

(25)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}}\sim_{\textit{hist}}\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-IC-N}}}\text{ where }\textit{hist}\text{ is a function such that }

\displaystyle\langle\forall s,h\colon\langle s,h\rangle\in S_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\colon\textit{hist}(\langle s,h\rangle)=s\rangle

(26)

\langle\forall s\colon s\in S^{\textit{init}}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}\colon\langle s,\textit{init-h}_{\texttt{MA-IC}}(s)\rangle\in S^{\textit{ent}}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\rangle

(27)

\langle\forall s\colon s\in S^{\textit{ent}}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\colon\langle\forall w\colon s\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-IC-H}]{}w\colon w\in S^{\textit{ent}}_{\texttt{MA-IC-H}}\rangle\rangle

In addition, our notion of correctness for Meltdown requires that

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
is a witness skipping refinement of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
with
respect to our refinement map
r-ic
, defined below. This
is proved by showing the existence of a witness skipping relation on
the transition system produced by taking the “disjoint union” of

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
. Let

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\textit{ic}}}=\langle S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}\uplus S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}},\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-G-IC}]{}\uplus\xrightarrow[\texttt{ISA-IC}]{},\mathcal{L}\rangle
be this system. Let

S_{\textit{ic}}=S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}\uplus S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}
and

\xrightarrow[\textit{ic}]{}=\xrightarrow[\texttt{MA-G-IC}]{}\uplus\xrightarrow[\texttt{ISA-IC}]{}
. We
instantiate Definition
2.5
, providing

\textit{skip-wit-ic}:S_{\textit{ic}}\times S_{\textit{ic}}\rightarrow\mathbb{N}\setminus\{0\}
for
skip-wit
,

\textit{stutter-wit-ic}:S_{\textit{ic}}\times S_{\textit{ic}}\rightarrow\mathbb{N}
for
stutter-wit
,

\textit{run-ic}:S_{\textit{ic}}\times S_{\textit{ic}}\times S_{\textit{ic}}\rightarrow S_{\textit{ic}}
for
run
,
and

B_{\textit{ic}}\subseteq S_{\textit{ic}}\times S_{\textit{ic}}

for
B
. The obligations generated are as follows:

(28)

\langle\forall s\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}::sB_{\textit{ic}}\textit{r-ic}.s\rangle

(29)

\langle\forall w,s,u\colon sB_{\textit{ic}}w\wedge s\xrightarrow[\textit{ic}]{}u\colon w\xrightarrow[\textit{ic}]{}^{\textit{skip-wit-ic}(s,u)}\textit{run-ic}(w,s,u)\rangle

(30)

\displaystyle\forall s,u,w\in S_{\textit{ic}}:sB_{\textit{ic}}w\,\wedge\,s\xrightarrow[\textit{ic}]{}u:

\displaystyle\text{\quad}(uB_{\textit{ic}}w\,\wedge\,\textit{stutter-wit-ic}(u,w)<\textit{stutter-wit-ic}(s,w))\ \vee

\displaystyle\text{\quad}uB_{\textit{ic}}(\textit{run-ic}(w,s,u))

Recall that

S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}:\langle\textbf{pc},\textbf{rf},\textbf{tsx},\textbf{halt},\textbf{imem},\textbf{dmem},\textbf{ga},\textbf{cache}\rangle

The refinement map for
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
and label function for
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}

are as follows:

\textit{r-ic}(\langle s,h\rangle)=\langle\textbf{pc}_{s},\textbf{rf}_{s},\textbf{tsx}_{s},\textbf{halt}_{s},\textbf{imem}_{s},\textbf{dmem}_{s},\textbf{ga}_{s},\emptyset\rangle

L_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}(s)=[\textbf{cache}\mapsto\emptyset]s

We then define
B_{\textit{ic}}
in the following way:

\displaystyle B_{\textit{ic}}(s,w)\iff

\displaystyle\begin{cases}s=w&\text{if }s,w\in S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}\vee s,w\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}\\
L_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}(s)=L_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}(\textit{r-ic}(w))&\text{if }s\in S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}\wedge w\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}\\
L_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}(\textit{r-ic}(s))=L_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}(w)&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

Note that
B_{\textit{ic}}
and the above obligations are stated in a
way that is agnostic of whether the two related states
s
and
w
are
both from
S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}
or
S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}
, or whether they are from
different systems. For the sake of brevity, we will only give a short
discussion regarding handling the case where the two states are in the
same system: for all
s,w\in S_{\textit{ic}}
such that

s\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}\wedge w\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}
or

s\in S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}\wedge w\in S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}
, the following hold:

\textit{skip-wit-ic}(s,w)=1
and

\textit{stutter-wit-ic}(s,w)=0
.

We now discuss the behavior when the two states are in different
systems. We focus primarily on the case where
s\in S_{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}
and

w\in S_{\texttt{ISA-IC}}
.

We define
\textit{stutter-wit-ic}(s,w)
to be a function that
returns the number of steps it will take starting at the state
s

before at least one instruction is retired. By inspecting the
transitions of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
it is straightforward to produce a method
for computing this value.

\textit{skip-wit-ic}(s,u)
is a function that returns the number
of instructions that are committed in the transition from
s
to

u
. This is exactly the number of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
steps that should be
required to match the behavior of the
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
step.

\textit{run-ic}(w,s,u)
is a function that steps
w

\textit{skip-wit-ic}(s,u)
times, using
s
and
u
to resolve
nondeterminism when there are multiple successors to the
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}

state. The goal is, for each instruction, ensure that the

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{ISA-IC}}}
’s cache prior to executing that instruction is equivalent
to the cache that the
\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
had when that instruction was
executed. This can be gleaned from the history information gathered by

\mathcal{M}_{\mathit{\texttt{MA-G-IC}}}
. Once the desired cache state prior to instruction
execution is known, it is possible to choose the first

isa-ic-c
transition that is part of an
isa-ic

transition in such a way that the desired cache state is achieved
prior to instruction execution. A similar technique can be used to
determine what the state of the cache should be after each instruction
is executed, so the second
isa-ic-c
transition can be chosen
to achieve it.
